│     • Usage: python /app/decompress.py &lt;resharded_dir&gt;                 │

╭──────────────────── logging - explorer - system_message ─────────────────────╮
│                                                                              │
│                                   Context                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • You are a helpful agent part of a Multi Agent System that solves server   │
│    and command-line related tasks.                                           │
│  • You, all other agents and the user operate on the same system.            │
│  • Once the user provides a task, the Multi Agent System's goal is to        │
│    complete the task collaboratively, without returning to the user for      │
│    clarification.- The chat history includes the user's task, progress       │
│    summaries, and information from previous agents, such as previous         │
│    sub-task plans, feedback, executed steps, exploration details and more.   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Role: you are the Explorer agent of the Multi Agent System.               │
│  • Based on the provided task by the user, use the given tools to gather     │
│    some basic information relevant to completing the task.                   │
│  • It is possible that the last prior agent has left instructions for you in │
│    latest message with open topics that still need to be explored. If so,    │
│    prioritize answering the other agents' exploration requests.              │
│  • Do not try to solve the tasks, instead, collect only basic information    │
│    that will assist later agents in planning and actively solving the task.  │
│  • When finished write a concise summary of only the gathered information    │
│    and include the tag '[subtaskplanner]'.                                   │
│  • Include a tag in your answer only when you intend to pass it on.          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                         Code Execution Capabilities                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • Your are equipped with different tools for executing code.                │
│  • Do only use the tools to execute code. Code inside a Markdown Code Block  │
│    will not be executed.                                                     │
│  • Use Shell tool for simple, single bash commands. Do not use it for        │
│    commands that open interactive terminal programs (e.g. git log or man).   │
│  • Use Terminal tool to execute commands in a persistent terminal instance   │
│    and get back the exact terminal screen. This is especially useful for     │
│    interactive programs such as git log, man, or vim.                        │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                             More Operating Rules                             │
│                                                                              │
│  • Think and act step by step and explain your actions and reasoning.        │
│  • Break complex objectives into smaller sub-goals if necessary.             │
│  • Use tools to gather knowledge or verify assumptions before proceeding.    │
│  • If you call a tool it will be executed and results will be returned to    │
│    you.                                                                      │

│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Role: you are the Subtaskplanner agent of the Multi Agent System.         │
│  • Your job is to define the next reasonable and small sub-task of the       │
│    overall user task and provide a high-level plan outline of how it could   │
│    be solved.                                                                │
│  • The Subtasksolver agent will carry out the sub-task, you should only      │
│    focus on determine the next appropriate sub-task and a simple plan        │
│    sketch.                                                                   │
│  • Make sure the sub-task and plan sketch describe only the next reasonable  │
│    step forward, keeping in mind that some work may already have been        │
│    completed by previous agents.                                             │
│  • The sub-task should be described concisely in verbal form and make sure   │
│    the plan outline is high-level only and not a detailed breakdown.         │
│  • If you can not provide the next sub-task and need more system or          │
│    environment information first, specify exactly what you need and include  │
│    the tag '[explorer]'.                                                     │
│  • If you want your finalized sub-task carried out, you must describe it,    │
│    give a high-level plan sketch, and also include the tag                   │
│    '[subtasksolver]'.                                                        │
│  • If no further sub-tasks are needed and the overall user task is complete, │
│    respond only with the tag '[tester]'.                                     │
│  • Include a tag in your answer only when you intend to pass it on.          │

│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Role: you are the Subtasksolver agent of the Multi Agent System.          │
│  • Your job is to actively complete only the most recently defined sub-task, │
│    using the available tools.                                                │
│  • The previous Subtaskplanner agent message includes the sub-task for you   │
│    to actively complete, and an optional plan sketch you may follow.         │
│  • Attention: you do not have to follow the proposed plan if you think you   │
│    have a better idea  but always keep your focus on the sub-task.           │
│  • Continue working until the described sub-task is done or no further       │
│    contribution is possible.                                                 │
│  • Also, verify whether the chosen sub-task is reasonable. If it needs       │
│    refinement, provide your feedback and include the tag '[subtaskplanner]'. │
│  • When finished, or you get stuck, write a summary of what you did and      │
│    include the tag '[subtaskplanner]'.                                       │
│  • Include a tag in your answer only when you intend to pass it on.          │

│                         Code Execution Capabilities                          │

│    will not be executed.                                                     │
│  • Use Shell tool for simple, single bash commands. Do not use it for        │
│    commands that open interactive terminal programs (e.g. git log or man).   │
│  • Use PythonExecutor tool for complex logic, scripting, or data processing. │
│  • Use Terminal tool to execute commands in a persistent terminal instance   │
│    and get back the exact terminal screen. This is especially useful for     │
│    interactive programs such as git log, man, or vim.                        │

│    interactive programs such as git log, man, or vim.                        │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                             More Operating Rules                             │
│                                                                              │
│  • Think and act step by step and explain your actions and reasoning.        │
│  • Break complex objectives into smaller sub-goals if necessary.             │
│  • Use tools to gather knowledge or verify assumptions before proceeding.    │
│  • If you call a tool it will be executed and results will be returned to    │
│    you.                                                                      │
╰──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
╭──────────────────── logging - approver - system_message ─────────────────────╮
│                                                                              │
│                                   Context                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • You are a helpful agent part of a Multi Agent System that solves server   │
│    and command-line related tasks.                                           │
│  • You, all other agents and the user operate on the same system.            │
│  • Once the user provides a task, the Multi Agent System's goal is to        │
│    complete the task collaboratively, without returning to the user for      │
│    clarification.- The chat history includes the user's task, progress       │
│    summaries, and information from previous agents, such as previous         │
│    sub-task plans, feedback, executed steps, exploration details and more.   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Your role is the "Approver" agent of the Multi Agent System.              │
│  • Your job is to determine if the agents have done enough work on the task. │
│  • Make your decision based on all information gathered and the actions      │
│    performed by previous agents.                                             │
│  • Be critical and selective, approve only what you are completely sure of.  │
│  • If you decide that the task was not solved successfully, briefly explain  │
│    why and include the tag '[refiner]'.                                      │
│  • If you decide that not enough testing was done to ensure the task is      │
│    fully and successfully completed, briefly explain why and add the tag     │
│    '[tester]'.                                                               │
│  • If you decide that sufficient work was done, just reply briefly with the  │
│    tag '[summarizer]'.                                                       │
│  • Make one of the three decisions and include its tag in your response.     │
╰──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
╭─────────────────── logging - summarizer - system_message ────────────────────╮
│                                                                              │
│                                   Context                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • You are a helpful agent part of a Multi Agent System that solves server   │

│  • Once the user provides a task, the Multi Agent System's goal is to        │
│    complete the task collaboratively, without returning to the user for      │
│    clarification.- The chat history includes the user's task, progress       │
│    summaries, and information from previous agents, such as previous         │
│    sub-task plans, feedback, executed steps, exploration details and more.   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Your role is the "Summarizer" agent of the Multi Agent System.            │
│  • Your job is to write the final concise summary of the entire task         │
│    completion process.                                                       │
│  • Your summary should briefly describe the solution plan, its execution,    │
│    and the results of testing.                                               │
│  • Highlight key findings or lessons learned, if any.                        │

│ output_dir/\n      - group_0/\n        - shard_000/\n          -             │
│ file_000.jsonl\n          - file_001.jsonl\n          - ...\n          -     │
│ file_029.jsonl  (up to 30 files in this shard)\n          - manifest.json    │
│ (mapping for this shard)\n        - shard_001/\n        - ...\n        -     │
│ shard_029/\n      - group_1/\n        - shard_000/\n        - ...\n      -   │
│ ...\n  - Top-level output_dir will therefore have up to 30 groups (we’ll cap │
│ the number of groups to keep top-level under 30, but for a dataset this size │
│ even 11 groups is enough: 9898 files / (30 files/shard * 30 shards/group) =  │
│ ~11 groups).\n  - This ensures:\n    - Top-level directory (output_dir) has  │
│ ≤ 30 entries (group_0, group_1, ..., group_10, etc.).\n    - Each group      │
│ directory has ≤ 30 shard directories.\n    - Each shard directory has ≤ 30   │
│ files.\n  - Each shard will contain a manifest.json that records, for every  │
│ stored file, the original relative path from the input_dir and the           │
│ shard-local filename. This is essential for exact decompression.\n\n-        │
│ compress.py design (conceptual)\n  - Gather a deterministic list of input    │
│ files under input_dir (regular files only, recursively discovered; sort by   │
│ path for reproducibility).\n  - Compute grouping:\n    - total_files = N\n   │
│ - files_per_shard = 30\n    - shards_per_group = 30\n    - files_per_group = │
│ shards_per_group * files_per_shard = 900\n    - group_count = ceil(N /       │
│ 900)\n  - Create output_dir structure:\n    - For g in 0..group_count-1:\n   │
│ - create output_dir/group_g\n      - For s in 0..29 (shards_per_group):\n    │
│ - create output_dir/group_g/shard_s\n        - This shard will hold up to 30 │
│ files (or fewer if fewer remaining)\n      - Each shard contains:\n        - │
│ up to 30 files copied from input, preserving content (use copy2).\n        - │
│ a manifest.json with entries like:\n          { "src_rel":                   │
│ "<relative_path_in_input_dir>", "dst_file":                                  │
│ "group_g/shard_s/file_<idx>.<ext>" }\n    - A top-level file root_path.txt   │
│ or a base marker to record the original input_dir root, enabling decompress  │
│ to know where to restore files.\n  - File naming inside shards:\n    - Use   │
│ stable, unique names such as file_00000.ext, file_00001.ext, etc., to avoid  │
│ collisions within a shard.\n  - File size constraint:\n    - If any input    │
│ file exceeds 15 MB, we should split it or raise a clear error.\n    - Given  │
│ the current dataset (JSONL files around tens to hundreds of KB), this        │
│ constraint will not be violated in practice for this slice.\n  - Output      │
│ verification (optional quick check):\n    - Ensure each shard directory has  │
│ ≤ 30 files and each group directory ≤ 30 shards, etc.\n\n- decompress.py     │
│ design (conceptual)\n  - Read resharded_dir and the manifest.json files in   │
│ each shard.\n  - Use root_path.txt (written by compress.py) to determine the │
│ original input_dir root path.\n  - For each shard manifest entry:\n    -     │
│ Recreate the original directory structure under the original input root (as  │
│ indicated by src_rel).\n    - Copy the shard file (dst_file) back to         │
│ input_dir/src_rel, preserving file content exactly (copy2 to preserve        │
│ metadata if desired).\n  - After processing all shards:\n    - Remove the    │
│ resharded_dir (delete the group_/shard_ tree) to perform the “in-place”      │
│ restoration.\n  - Integrity checks (optional):\n    - After decompress,      │
│ compare checksums or file sizes of original files with the reconstituted     │
│ ones to ensure exact reconstruction.\n\n- Testing plan (on c4_sample)\n  -   │
│ Step 1: Prepare\n    - Create /app/pyproject.toml with a minimal uv project  │
│ configuration.\n    - Create a /app/venv via uv (per uv workflow) or         │

│ enabling decompress to reconstruct in-place without extra user input.\n  -   │

│ data to reshard")\n    parser.add_argument("output_dir", help="Output        │
│ directory for resharded data (will be created if missing)")\n    return      │
│ parser.parse_args()\n\n\ndef collect_files(input_dir: str):\n    items = []  │
│ # (rel_path, full_path, size)\n    input_dir = os.path.abspath(input_dir)\n  │
│ for root, , files in os.walk(input_dir, topdown=True, followlinks=False):\n  │
│ for name in files:\n            full = os.path.join(root, name)\n            │
│ # Only regular files\n            if not os.path.isfile(full) or             │
│ os.path.islink(full):\n                continue\n            try:\n          │
│ size = os.path.getsize(full)\n            except OSError:\n                  │
│ continue\n            rel = os.path.relpath(full, input_dir)\n               │
│ rel = rel.replace(os.sep, "/")  # use POSIX style in manifests\n             │
│ items.append((rel, full, size))\n    # Deterministic order for               │
│ reproducibility\n    items.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])\n    return              │
│ items\n\n\ndef ensure_file_size_limit(size: int, limit: int = 15 * 1024 *    │
│ 1024) -> None:\n    if size > limit:\n        raise ValueError(f"File size   │
│ {size} bytes exceeds per-file limit of {limit} bytes")\n\n\ndef main():\n    │
│ args = parse_args()\n    input_dir = os.path.abspath(args.input_dir)\n       │
│ output_dir = os.path.abspath(args.output_dir)\n\n    if not                  │
│ os.path.isdir(input_dir):\n        print(f"Error: input_dir does not exist   │
│ or is not a directory: {input_dir}", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(2)\n │
│ os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Collect and validate input   │
│ files\n    files = collect_files(input_dir)\n    N = len(files)\n    #       │
│ Enforce per-file size limit\n    try:\n        for rel, full, size in        │
│ files:\n            ensure_file_size_limit(size, 15 * 1024 * 1024)\n         │
│ except ValueError as e:\n        print(f"Error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)\n     │
│ sys.exit(3)\n\n    files_per_shard = 30\n    shards_per_group = 30\n         │
│ files_per_group = files_per_shard * shards_per_group  # 900\n\n              │
│ group_count = math.ceil(N / files_per_group) if N > 0 else 0\n\n    # Build  │
│ the directory layout and copy files\n    copied = 0\n    for gidx in         │
│ range(group_count):\n        group_dir = os.path.join(output_dir,            │
│ f"group{gidx}")\n        os.makedirs(group_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n           │
│ for sidx in range(shards_per_group):\n            shard_global = gidx *      │
│ shards_per_group + sidx\n            start = shard_global *                  │
│ files_per_shard\n            end = min(start + files_per_shard, N)\n         │
│ if start >= N:\n                break\n\n            shard_dir =             │
│ os.path.join(group_dir, f"shard_{sidx:03d}")\n                               │
│ os.makedirs(shard_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n            shard_entries = []\n    │
│ # Copy up to 30 files into this shard\n            for local_idx, (src_rel,  │
│ src_full, size) in enumerate(files[start:end], start=0):\n                   │
│ ext = Path(src_full).suffix\n                dst_name =                      │
│ f"file_{local_idx:05d}{ext}"\n                dst_path =                     │
│ os.path.join(shard_dir, dst_name)\n                shutil.copy2(src_full,    │
│ dst_path)\n                dst_rel = os.path.relpath(dst_path,               │
│ output_dir).replace(os.sep, "/")\n                                           │
│ shard_entries.append({"src_rel": src_rel, "dst_file": dst_rel})\n            │
│ copied += 1\n\n            # Write manifest for this shard\n            if   │
│ shard_entries:\n                manifest_path = os.path.join(shard_dir,      │
│ "manifest.json")\n                with open(manifest_path, "w",              │
│ encoding="utf-8") as mf:\n                    json.dump(shard_entries, mf,   │
│ indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n    # Write the root input_dir marker for   │
│ decompression\n    root_marker = os.path.join(output_dir, "root_path.txt")\n │
│ with open(root_marker, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:\n                        │
│ f.write(input_dir.rstrip("/\\") + "\n")\n\n    print("[done] compress.py     │
│ completed.")\n    print(f"Input files processed: {N}")\n    print(f"Output   │
│ layout groups: {group_count} (max 30 per group, 30 shards per group)")\n     │
│ print(f"Total files copied: {copied}")\n    print(f"Root marker:             │
│ {root_marker}")\n\n\nif name == "main":\n    main()\nPY\n\ncat >             │
│ /app/decompress.py <<"PY"\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n"""\nFull implementation  │
│ of the resharding decompress script.\n\nThis script reconstructs the         │
│ original directory layout in-place from a previously\ncreated resharded      │
│ directory. It reads per-shard manifests and a root_path marker\ncreated by   │
│ compress.py to restore files under the original input root.\nAfter           │
│ successful reconstruction, the resharded directory tree is                   │
│ removed.\n\nUsage:\n  python /app/decompress.py                              │
│ <resharded_dir>\n"""\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport      │
│ shutil\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n        │
│ parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        prog="decompress.py",\n           │
│ description="Reconstruct original data layout from a resharded directory",\n │
│ )\n    parser.add_argument("resharded_dir", help="Path to the resharded      │
│ directory produced by compress.py")\n    return parser.parse_args()\n\n\ndef │
│ main():\n    args = parse_args()\n    resharded_dir =                        │
│ os.path.abspath(args.resharded_dir)\n\n    if not                            │
│ os.path.isdir(resharded_dir):\n        print(f"Error: resharded_dir does not │
│ exist or is not a directory: {resharded_dir}", file=sys.stderr)\n            │
│ sys.exit(2)\n\n    root_marker = os.path.join(resharded_dir,                 │
│ "root_path.txt")\n    if not os.path.isfile(root_marker):\n                  │
│ print(f"Error: root_path marker not found in resharded_dir: {root_marker}",  │
│ file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(3)\n\n    with open(root_marker, "r",     │
│ encoding="utf-8") as f:\n        original_root = f.readline().strip()\n      │
│ if not original_root or not os.path.isdir(original_root):\n                  │
│ print(f"Error: original input root could not be resolved: {original_root}",  │
│ file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(4)\n\n    # Walk through all shard        │
│ manifests and copy files back to original_root/src_rel\n    files_restored = │
│ 0\n    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(resharded_dir):\n         │
│ if "manifest.json" in filenames:\n            manifest_path =                │
│ os.path.join(dirpath, "manifest.json")\n            with open(manifest_path, │
│ "r", encoding="utf-8") as mf:\n                entries = json.load(mf)\n     │
│ for entry in entries:\n                src_rel = entry.get("src_rel")\n      │
│ dst_file = entry.get("dst_file")\n                if not src_rel or not      │
│ dst_file:\n                    continue\n                src_file_path =     │
│ os.path.join(resharded_dir, dst_file)\n                dest_path =           │
│ os.path.join(original_root, src_rel)\n                dest_dir =             │
│ os.path.dirname(dest_path)\n                os.makedirs(dest_dir,            │
│ exist_ok=True)\n                if not os.path.isfile(src_file_path):\n      │
│ print(f"Warning: missing shard file {src_file_path}")\n                      │
│ continue\n                shutil.copy2(src_file_path, dest_path)\n           │
│ files_restored += 1\n\n    # Cleanup: remove resharded_dir after successful  │
│ restoration\n    try:\n        shutil.rmtree(resharded_dir)\n                │
│ print(f"Removed resharded directory: {resharded_dir}")\n    except Exception │
│ as e:\n        print(f"Warning: failed to remove resharded_dir: {e}")\n\n    │

│ 1024  # 15 MB\n\n\ndef compute_sha256(path: Path) -> str:\n    h =           │
│ hashlib.sha256()\n    with path.open(&#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:\n        for      │
│ chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(10241024), b&#x27;&#x27;):\n                    │
│ h.update(chunk)\n    return h.hexdigest()\n\n\ndef                           │

│ size)\n    base = input_dir.resolve()\n    for root, dirs, filenames in      │
│ os.walk(base):\n        for name in filenames:\n            p = Path(root) / │

│ hashlib.sha256()\n    with path.open(&#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:\n        for      │
│ chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(10241024), b&#x27;&#x27;):\n                    │
│ h.update(chunk)\n    return h.hexdigest()\n\n\ndef                           │

│ "root_path.txt").write_text(str(input_dir), encoding=&#x27;utf-8&#x27;)\n\n  │

│ MAX_FILE_SIZE:\n            print(f"Error: file {src_rel} exceeds max size   │

│ group = idx // FILES_PER_GROUP\n        within_group = idx %                 │
│ FILES_PER_GROUP\n        shard = within_group // MAX_FILES_PER_SHARD\n       │
│ local_idx = within_group % MAX_FILES_PER_SHARD\n        dst_dir = output_dir │

│ # Iterate shards and manifests\n    for group_dir in                         │

│ entries = json.load(mf)\n            for entry in entries:\n                 │
│ src_rel = entry["src_rel"]\n                dst_file_rel =                   │
│ entry["dst_file"]\n                # Actual destination path on original     │

│ dest_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n                        │

│ dst_file&#x27;s tail\n                # However, since we stored only        │

│ /app/decompress.py\n- Decompress resharded data back into the original input │

│ python3\n"""\nDecompress input_stage back into the original directory layout │
│ in-place.\n- Reads manifests from each shard and copies files back to their  │

│ main():\n    if len(sys.argv) != 2:\n        print("Usage: python            │

│ sorted(resharded_dir.glob("group_/")):\n        if not group_dir.is_dir():\n │

│ What I’m delivering here                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  • Full, robust implementations for:                                         │
│     • /app/compress.py                                                       │
│     • /app/decompress.py                                                     │
│  • Minimal uv project scaffolding so uv sync can create a venv in /app       │

│                                                                              │
│ Important notes                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  • The implementation strictly avoids creating empty shards. A shard         │
│    directory (group_x/shard_y) is created only when there is at least one    │
│    file assigned to it.                                                      │
│  • Each shard holds up to 30 files. Each group holds up to 30 shards, giving │
│    a maximum of 900 files per group. The directory tree is designed to       │
│    ensure no directory exceeds 30 entries.                                   │
│  • Each shard includes a manifest.json with a mapping for each stored file:  │
│     • src_rel: the file’s original path relative to the input root.          │
│     • dst_file: the path of the shard file relative to the resharded_dir (so │
│       decompression can locate the shard file reliably).                     │
│     • Optional sha256: if you run with --verify, the manifest will include a │
│       sha256 hash to enable integrity checks during decompression.           │
│  • A root_path.txt marker is written in the output_dir to capture the        │
│    absolute original input_dir path; decompress uses this to reconstruct in  │
│    the original root in-place.                                               │
│  • The decompress step copies files back from                                │
│    resharded_dir/group_x/shard_y/... to original_root/src_rel and, if        │
│    verification data exists, validates the SHA-256 hash. It then cleans up   │
│    the resharded_dir.                                                        │
│  • The solution is designed to be generic enough to handle other slices with │
│    similar structure and distribution, as long as input_dir contains regular │
│    files and per-file size constraints are respected.                        │
│                                                                              │
│ Code: /app/pyproject.toml                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • Minimal uv project manifest so uv sync can create a venv in /app.         │

│ Code: /app/compress.py                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • Deterministic file collection, grouping into shards and groups.           │
│  • Per-shard manifest.json containing mappings.                              │
│  • Root marker (root_path.txt) storing original input_dir.                   │
│  • Optional --verify flag to compute and store SHA-256 per input file.       │
│  • No empty shards created; shards are created lazily when needed.           │
│  • 15 MB per-file size cap enforced.                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Compress input_dir into a resharded structure     │
│ under output_dir.                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│  • Each shard contains up to 30 files.                                       │
│  • Each group contains up to 30 shards (thus up to 900 files per group).     │
│  • Directories will never be empty (shards are created only when files are   │
│    assigned).                                                                │
│  • File size per input file must be <= 15 MB.                                │
│  • Command: python /app/compress.py <input_dir> <output_dir> [--verify] """  │
│    import argparse import json import hashlib import os import shutil import │
│    sys from pathlib import Path                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ MAX_FILES_PER_SHARD = 30 SHARDS_PER_GROUP = 30 FILES_PER_GROUP =             │
│ MAX_FILES_PER_SHARD * SHARDS_PER_GROUP  # 900 MAX_FILE_SIZE = 15 * 1024 *    │
│ 1024  # 15 MB                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ def compute_sha256(path: Path) -> str: h = hashlib.sha256() with             │
│ path.open("rb") as f: for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b""):   │
│ h.update(chunk) return h.hexdigest()                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ def list_input_files(input_dir: Path): files = []  # (rel_path, abs_path,    │
│ size) base = input_dir.resolve() for root, dirs, filenames in os.walk(base): │
│ for name in filenames: p = Path(root) / name if not p.is_file(): continue    │
│ rel = p.relative_to(base).as_posix() size = p.stat().st_size                 │
│ files.append((rel, str(p), size)) # deterministic order                      │
│ files.sort(key=lambda x: x[0]) return files                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Compress input_dir │
│ into resharded structure under output_dir.")                                 │
│ parser.add_argument("input_dir", type=str, help="Source input directory      │
│ containing files to reshard") parser.add_argument("output_dir", type=str,    │
│ help="Destination directory to write resharded shards")                      │
│ parser.add_argument("--verify", action="store_true", help="Compute and store │
│ SHA-256 for each input file for integrity verification during decompress")   │
│ args = parser.parse_args()                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  input_dir = Path(args.input_dir).resolve()                                  │
│  if not input_dir.exists() or not input_dir.is_dir():                        │
│      print(f&quot;Error: input_dir does not exist or is not a directory:     │
│  {input_dir}&quot;)                                                          │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  output_dir = Path(args.output_dir).resolve()                                │
│  # Fresh start: clean if exists but only when explicitly allowed; to be      │
│  safe, refuse if non-empty                                                   │
│  if output_dir.exists() and any(output_dir.iterdir()):                       │
│      print(f&quot;Error: output_dir {output_dir} already exists and is not   │
│  empty. Refuse to overwrite to avoid data loss.&quot;)                       │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)                               │
│                                                                              │
│  files = list_input_files(input_dir)                                         │
│  total = len(files)                                                          │
│  if total == 0:                                                              │
│      print(&quot;No input files found; producing empty output.&quot;)        │
│      (output_dir / &quot;root_path.txt&quot;).write_text(str(input_dir),     │
│  encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;)                                                 │
│      return                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Track per-shard writes                                                    │
│  shard_entries = {}  # (group, shard) -&gt; list of entries                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # root path marker                                                          │
│  (output_dir / &quot;root_path.txt&quot;).write_text(str(input_dir),         │
│  encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  for idx, (src_rel, abs_path, size) in enumerate(files):                     │
│      if size &gt; MAX_FILE_SIZE:                                             │
│          print(f&quot;Error: file {src_rel} exceeds max size                 │
│  {MAX_FILE_SIZE} bytes ({size} bytes)&quot;)                                 │
│          sys.exit(1)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      group = idx // FILES_PER_GROUP                                          │
│      within_group = idx % FILES_PER_GROUP                                    │
│      shard = within_group // MAX_FILES_PER_SHARD                             │
│      local_idx = within_group % MAX_FILES_PER_SHARD                          │
│                                                                              │
│      dst_dir = output_dir / f&quot;group_{group}&quot; /                     │
│  f&quot;shard_{shard}&quot;                                                  │
│      if not dst_dir.exists():                                                │
│          dst_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)                          │
│                                                                              │
│      dst_file = dst_dir /                                                    │
│  f&quot;file_{local_idx:05d}{Path(abs_path).suffix}&quot;                    │
│                                                                              │
│      sha = None                                                              │
│      if args.verify:                                                         │
│          sha = compute_sha256(Path(abs_path))                                │
│                                                                              │
│      # Copy the file                                                         │
│      shutil.copy2(abs_path, dst_file)                                        │
│                                                                              │
│      key = (group, shard)                                                    │
│      if key not in shard_entries:                                            │
│          shard_entries[key] = []                                             │
│      entry = {                                                               │
│          &quot;src_rel&quot;: src_rel,                                       │
│          &quot;dst_file&quot;: str(Path(f&quot;group_{group}&quot;) /        │
│  Path(f&quot;shard_{shard}&quot;) / Path(dst_file.name)),                    │
│      }                                                                       │
│      if sha is not None:                                                     │
│          entry[&quot;sha256&quot;] = sha                                     │
│      shard_entries[key].append(entry)                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  # write manifests per shard                                                 │
│  for (g, s), entries in shard_entries.items():                               │
│      shard_dir = output_dir / f&quot;group_{g}&quot; /                       │
│  f&quot;shard_{s}&quot;                                                      │
│      manifest_path = shard_dir / &quot;manifest.json&quot;                   │
│      with manifest_path.open(&quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as   │
│  mf:                                                                         │
│          json.dump(entries, mf, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)                │
│                                                                              │
│  print(f&quot;Compressed {total} files into resharded structure under        │
│  {output_dir}&quot;)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ if name == "main": main()                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ Code: /app/decompress.py                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  • Decompress resharded data back into the original input directory          │
│    structure in-place.                                                       │
│  • Reads manifests from each shard and copies files back to their original   │
│    relative paths.                                                           │
│  • Removes the resharded directory tree after successful decompression.      │
│  • Usage: python /app/decompress.py <resharded_dir>                          │
│                                                                              │
│ #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Decompress input_stage back into the original     │
│ directory layout in-place.                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  • Reads manifests from each shard and copies files back to their original   │
│    relative paths.                                                           │
│  • Removes the resharded_dir tree after success.                             │
│  • Usage: python /app/decompress.py <resharded_dir> """ import json import   │
│    shutil import sys from pathlib import Path                                │
│                                                                              │
│ def compute_sha256(path: Path) -> str: import hashlib h = hashlib.sha256()   │
│ with path.open("rb") as f: for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024),    │
│ b""): h.update(chunk) return h.hexdigest()                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): if len(sys.argv) != 2: print("Usage: python /app/decompress.py   │
│ <resharded_dir>") sys.exit(1)                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  resharded_dir = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()                                 │
│  if not resharded_dir.exists() or not resharded_dir.is_dir():                │
│      print(f&quot;Error: resharped_dir does not exist or is not a            │
│  directory: {resharded_dir}&quot;)                                           │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  root_marker = resharded_dir / &quot;root_path.txt&quot;                     │
│  if not root_marker.exists():                                                │
│      print(&quot;Error: root_path.txt marker not found in resharded_dir;     │
│  cannot determine original root.&quot;)                                      │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│  original_root =                                                             │
│  Path(root_marker.read_text(encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;).strip())             │
│  if not original_root.exists():                                              │
│      print(f&quot;Error: original root path from root_path.txt does not      │
│  exist: {original_root}&quot;)                                               │
│      sys.exit(1)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  # Iterate shards and manifests                                              │
│  for group_dir in sorted(resharded_dir.glob(&quot;group_*/&quot;)):          │
│      if not group_dir.is_dir():                                              │
│          continue                                                            │
│      for shard_dir in sorted(group_dir.glob(&quot;shard_*/&quot;)):          │
│          if not shard_dir.is_dir():                                          │
│              continue                                                        │
│          manifest_path = shard_dir / &quot;manifest.json&quot;               │
│          if not manifest_path.exists():                                      │
│              continue                                                        │
│          with manifest_path.open(&quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;)  │
│  as mf:                                                                      │
│              entries = json.load(mf)                                         │
│          for entry in entries:                                               │
│              src_rel = entry[&quot;src_rel&quot;]                            │
│              dst_file_rel = entry[&quot;dst_file&quot;]  # relative to       │
│  resharded_dir                                                               │
│              dest_path = original_root / src_rel                             │
│              dest_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)             │
│              src_path = resharded_dir / Path(dst_file_rel)                   │
│              if not src_path.exists():                                       │
│                  print(f&quot;Warning: expected shard file not found:        │
│  {src_path}&quot;)                                                           │
│                  continue                                                    │
│              shutil.copy2(src_path, dest_path)                               │
│                                                                              │
│              # Integrity check (if present)                                  │
│              sha = entry.get(&quot;sha256&quot;)                             │
│              if sha is not None:                                             │
│                  rebuilt = compute_sha256(dest_path)                         │
│                  if rebuilt != sha:                                          │
│                      print(f&quot;Integrity check failed for {dest_path}     │
│  (expected {sha}, got {rebuilt})&quot;)                                      │
│                      sys.exit(1)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  # Cleanup resharded_dir                                                     │
│  shutil.rmtree(resharded_dir, ignore_errors=True)                            │
│  print(f&quot;Decompression complete. Restored data under {original_root}    │
│  and removed {resharded_dir}.&quot;)                                         │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ if name == "main": main()                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ Code: /app/README.md                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • Short notes about the workspace, usage, and testing. Also document the    │
│    verification option and test harness availability.                        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                         Reshard Tools (in /app)                          ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│  • Usage:                                                                    │
│     • python /app/compress.py <input_dir> <output_dir> [--verify]            │
│     • python /app/decompress.py <resharded_dir>                              │
│  • uv sync: to create and pin a venv in /app, run:                           │
│     • uv sync                                                                │
│  • Verification:                                                             │
│     • Use --verify on compress.py to store SHA-256 hashes and verify during  │
│       decompress.py.                                                         │
│  • Test harness:                                                             │
│     • /app/tests/test_cycle.py contains a small end-to-end test using a      │
│       temporary sample.                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Code: /app/tests/test_cycle.py                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Lightweight end-to-end harness to validate a tiny compress/decompress     │
│    cycle.                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ #!/usr/bin/env python3 import os import sys import tempfile import           │
│ subprocess from pathlib import Path                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ THIS_PY = sys.executable                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ def run(cmd, cwd=None): print(f"RUN: {cmd}") res = subprocess.run(cmd,       │
│ shell=True, cwd=cwd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,       │
│ text=True) print(res.stdout) if res.returncode != 0: raise                   │
│ SystemExit(res.returncode)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ def test_cycle_basic(): with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as src_root,      │
│ tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as out_root: src = Path(src_root) out =        │
│ Path(out_root) # Create a tiny sample with a few files files = { "a.txt":    │
│ b"hello world\n", "sub/b.json": b"{&quot;v&quot;:1}\n", "sub/c.txt":         │
│ b"another file", "nested/d.csv": b"id,name\n1,A\n2,B\n", } for rel, data in  │
│ files.items(): p = src / rel p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)     │
│ p.write_bytes(data)                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      # Run compress                                                          │
│      run(f&quot;{THIS_PY} /app/compress.py {src} {out} --verify&quot;,       │
│  cwd=&quot;/app&quot;)                                                       │
│      # Decompress back into the original root                                │
│      run(f&quot;{THIS_PY} /app/decompress.py {out}&quot;,                    │
│  cwd=&quot;/app&quot;)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      # Validate: content should match original                               │
│      for rel, data in files.items():                                         │
│          p = src / rel                                                       │
│          current = p.read_bytes()                                            │
│          if current != data:                                                 │
│              raise SystemExit(f&quot;Mismatch for {rel}&quot;)               │
│      print(&quot;End-to-end test cycle passed.&quot;)                        │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ if name == "main": test_cycle_basic()                                        │

│ <<&#x27;PY&#x27;\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\ncompress.py\nUsage: python │
│ /app/compress.py <input_dir> <output_dir> [--verify]\n- Collects all regular │
│ files under input_dir (recursively), sorts by path, and shards them into\n   │
│ group_/shard_/files, with up to 30 files per shard and up to 30 shards per   │
│ group.\n- Writes a manifest.json per shard describing original src_rel and   │
│ dst_file (relative to output_dir).\n- Creates root_path.txt in output_dir    │
│ containing the absolute input_dir path for decompression.\n- If --verify is  │
│ provided, computes sha256 for each input file and stores a &#x27;hash&#x27;  │
│ field in the manifest.\n- Skips creating empty shards.\n\"\"\"\nimport       │
│ sys\nimport os\nimport shutil\nimport json\nimport hashlib\nfrom pathlib     │
│ import Path\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Optional,                        │
│ Tuple\n\nFILES_PER_SHARD = 30\nSHARDS_PER_GROUP = 30\nMAX_FILE_SIZE = 15 *   │
│ 1024 * 1024  # 15 MB\n\n\ndef sha256_of(path: Path) -> str:\n    h =         │
│ hashlib.sha256()\n    with path.open(&#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:\n        for      │
│ chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(10241024), b&#x27;&#x27;):\n                    │
│ h.update(chunk)\n    return h.hexdigest()\n\n\ndef collect_files(input_dir:  │
│ Path) -> List[Path]:\n    files = []\n    for p in                           │
│ sorted(input_dir.rglob(&#x27;&#x27;)):\n        if p.is_file():\n            │
│ files.append(p)\n    return files\n\n\ndef make_parent_dirs(p: Path):\n      │
│ p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n\ndef main():\n    import     │
│ argparse\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=&#x27;Reshard         │
│ compress: shard files into constrained directories.&#x27;)\n                 │
│ ap.add_argument(&#x27;input_dir&#x27;, type=str, help=&#x27;Input root       │
│ directory to compress&#x27;)\n    ap.add_argument(&#x27;output_dir&#x27;,    │
│ type=str, help=&#x27;Output root directory for resharded data&#x27;)\n       │
